CPU / CUDA 算術調査台帳 2026-11-12 (Terra、調査完了。未確認事項は各節に記載)
調査範囲: 保存済み研究の読み取り、nm/objdump/sha256による照合のみ。API、モデル、GPU計算、ビルド、再起動、設定変更、ベンチは実行していない。機密や会話内容を含むログは転記しない。
【事実】は示した保存物・コード又はコマンド結果、【推定】は条件を添えた静的な導出、【未確認】は動的計測等がなく判定しない事項。原文は該当絶対パスと元行番号。
【資料と状態】/home/codex/projects/llm-lab/AGENTS.md と二つの source AGENTS.md を先に読んだ。EVENING_RESULTS_JA.txtは研究完了と通常API final、hot14-reviewed実験構成である旨を記録している。
【事実・親からの限定読取】hot14-reviewed.jsonは通常sandboxでPermissionError。sudo -nはno new privilegesのため失敗。親agentがrequire_escalatedの読み取りのみでプロファイルを抽出し、次を通知: GGML_XEON_HOT_FFN=1、GGML_XEON_HOT_PLAN=hot-ffn/hot-14GiB.plan、GGML_CPU_IQ_PLAN=hot-ffn/cpu40-without-hot-14GiB.plan、64/64 offload閾値。下のlibrary3つを指定。selected_projections=19200は旧継承値であり実planを基準。私は当該private profile本文を直接読んでいない。
【事実】final.jsonは37 CPU expert layers、37-39 GPU。freezeされたhot14生成スクリプトは元finalのLD_LIBRARY_PATHにhot libllamaを前置し、40 CPU層、GPU expert layers空、除外済CPU planに変更。hot14-reviewed自体の出所は親の限定読取に基づく。hot14-reviewed自体を今回実行していない。別名hot14-traceで行った過去pilotの/proc mapsは保存済みで、以下にそのlibrary一覧を示す。

実plan先頭 /home/codex/projects/llm-lab/exploration-20261112/hot-ffn/selected-14GiB.json : {

=== B. グラフ、レジデンシ、ルーティングおよびweighted tail ===
【事実】private GraphはF32入力、I32 IDs、gate/up/downの3 mul_mat_id、swiglu_clampを構成。weightsは各元量子化typeのままexpertバイト列をコピー。TOP=6、D=5120、F=2304、E=384、layers=40。
【事実】hot CPU slotsは-1にし、残りのIDをそのままprivate CPUへ。GPUはローカルIDに縮約、非同期投入後にCPUを実行、GPUを同期し、GPU結果を元slotにmemcpy。演算値が違えば同一の順序での加重合成でも後続値が同じとは限らない。
【事実】条件を満たすT=1だけouter graph内をprivate providerに置換。関数がnullなら通常graph。routerの選択/weightsはouter graphに残る。hotの場合weighted multiplicationを明示的にouter GPUへ置く。後続6slotの加算はソース構築順0,1,2,3,4,5。加算ノードが最終的にどのbackendへ割当されたかは未確認、コードだけでGPUと断定しない。

=== C. CPU MUL_MAT_ID、入力量子化、compact/fallback ===
【事実】MXFP4 vec_dot_type=Q8_0; IQ2_S/IQ3_XXS/IQ3_S vec_dot_type=Q8_K。src1がvec_dot_typeでなければfrom_floatで変換。入力はFFNのup/gate前とdown前でそれぞれ変換される。
【事実】T=1、対象IQ型、!use_refでcache lookup。個々のexpert pointerがあればcompact_dot、なければtiled適用を試行後、通常type_traitsのdot。CPU hit/missはGPU hit/missとは別概念。-1のCPU無効slotはゼロを出力。

   302 | void quantize_row_q8_0(const float * GGML_RESTRICT x, void * GGML_RESTRICT vy, int64_t k) {
   303 |     assert(QK8_0 == 32);
   304 |     assert(k % QK8_0 == 0);
   305 |     const int nb = k / QK8_0;
   306 | 
   307 |     block_q8_0 * GGML_RESTRICT y = vy;
   308 | 
   309 | #if defined(__AVX2__) || defined(__AVX__)
   310 |     for (int i = 0; i < nb; i++) {
   311 |         // Load elements into 4 AVX vectors
   312 |         __m256 v0 = _mm256_loadu_ps( x );
   313 |         __m256 v1 = _mm256_loadu_ps( x + 8 );
   314 |         __m256 v2 = _mm256_loadu_ps( x + 16 );
   315 |         __m256 v3 = _mm256_loadu_ps( x + 24 );
   316 |         x += 32;
   317 | 
   318 |         // Compute max(abs(e)) for the block
   319 |         const __m256 signBit = _mm256_set1_ps( -0.0f );
   320 |         __m256 maxAbs = _mm256_andnot_ps( signBit, v0 );
   321 |         maxAbs = _mm256_max_ps( maxAbs, _mm256_andnot_ps( signBit, v1 ) );
   322 |         maxAbs = _mm256_max_ps( maxAbs, _mm256_andnot_ps( signBit, v2 ) );
   323 |         maxAbs = _mm256_max_ps( maxAbs, _mm256_andnot_ps( signBit, v3 ) );
   324 | 
   325 |         __m128 max4 = _mm_max_ps( _mm256_extractf128_ps( maxAbs, 1 ), _mm256_castps256_ps128( maxAbs ) );
   326 |         max4 = _mm_max_ps( max4, _mm_movehl_ps( max4, max4 ) );
   327 |         max4 = _mm_max_ss( max4, _mm_movehdup_ps( max4 ) );
   328 |         const float maxScalar = _mm_cvtss_f32( max4 );
   329 | 
   330 |         // Quantize these floats
   331 |         const float d = maxScalar / 127.f;
   332 |         y[i].d = GGML_CPU_FP32_TO_FP16(d);
   333 |         const float id = ( maxScalar != 0.0f ) ? 127.f / maxScalar : 0.0f;
   334 |         const __m256 mul = _mm256_set1_ps( id );
   335 | 
   336 |         // Apply the multiplier
   337 |         v0 = _mm256_mul_ps( v0, mul );
   338 |         v1 = _mm256_mul_ps( v1, mul );
   339 |         v2 = _mm256_mul_ps( v2, mul );
   340 |         v3 = _mm256_mul_ps( v3, mul );
   341 | 
   342 |         // Round to nearest integer
   343 |         v0 = _mm256_round_ps( v0, _MM_ROUND_NEAREST );
   344 |         v1 = _mm256_round_ps( v1, _MM_ROUND_NEAREST );
   345 |         v2 = _mm256_round_ps( v2, _MM_ROUND_NEAREST );
   346 |         v3 = _mm256_round_ps( v3, _MM_ROUND_NEAREST );
   347 | 
   348 |         // Convert floats to integers
   349 |         __m256i i0 = _mm256_cvtps_epi32( v0 );
   350 |         __m256i i1 = _mm256_cvtps_epi32( v1 );
   351 |         __m256i i2 = _mm256_cvtps_epi32( v2 );
   352 |         __m256i i3 = _mm256_cvtps_epi32( v3 );
   353 | 
   354 | #if defined(__AVX2__)
   355 |         // Convert int32 to int16
   356 |         i0 = _mm256_packs_epi32( i0, i1 );	// 0, 1, 2, 3,  8, 9, 10, 11,  4, 5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15
   357 |         i2 = _mm256_packs_epi32( i2, i3 );	// 16, 17, 18, 19,  24, 25, 26, 27,  20, 21, 22, 23, 28, 29, 30, 31
   358 |                                             // Convert int16 to int8
   359 |         i0 = _mm256_packs_epi16( i0, i2 );	// 0, 1, 2, 3,  8, 9, 10, 11,  16, 17, 18, 19,  24, 25, 26, 27,  4, 5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15, 20, 21, 22, 23, 28, 29, 30, 31
   360 | 
   361 |         // We got our precious signed bytes, but the order is now wrong
   362 |         // These AVX2 pack instructions process 16-byte pieces independently
   363 |         // The following instruction is fixing the order
   364 |         const __m256i perm = _mm256_setr_epi32( 0, 4, 1, 5, 2, 6, 3, 7 );
   365 |         i0 = _mm256_permutevar8x32_epi32( i0, perm );
   366 | 
   367 |         _mm256_storeu_si256((__m256i *)y[i].qs, i0);
   368 | #else
   369 |         // Since we don't have in AVX some necessary functions,
   370 |         // we split the registers in half and call AVX2 analogs from SSE
   371 |         __m128i ni0 = _mm256_castsi256_si128( i0 );
   372 |         __m128i ni1 = _mm256_extractf128_si256( i0, 1);
   373 |         __m128i ni2 = _mm256_castsi256_si128( i1 );
   374 |         __m128i ni3 = _mm256_extractf128_si256( i1, 1);
   375 |         __m128i ni4 = _mm256_castsi256_si128( i2 );
   376 |         __m128i ni5 = _mm256_extractf128_si256( i2, 1);
   377 |         __m128i ni6 = _mm256_castsi256_si128( i3 );
   378 |         __m128i ni7 = _mm256_extractf128_si256( i3, 1);
   379 | 
   380 |         // Convert int32 to int16
   381 |         ni0 = _mm_packs_epi32( ni0, ni1 );
   382 |         ni2 = _mm_packs_epi32( ni2, ni3 );
   383 |         ni4 = _mm_packs_epi32( ni4, ni5 );
   384 |         ni6 = _mm_packs_epi32( ni6, ni7 );
   385 |         // Convert int16 to int8
   386 |         ni0 = _mm_packs_epi16( ni0, ni2 );
   387 |         ni4 = _mm_packs_epi16( ni4, ni6 );
   388 | 
   389 |         _mm_storeu_si128((__m128i *)(y[i].qs +  0), ni0);
   390 |         _mm_storeu_si128((__m128i *)(y[i].qs + 16), ni4);
   391 | #endif
   392 |     }

   394 |     GGML_UNUSED(nb);
   395 |     // scalar

  ----- lines 273-295 -----
   273 | void quantize_row_q8_0_ref(const float * GGML_RESTRICT x, block_q8_0 * GGML_RESTRICT y, int64_t k) {
   274 |     assert(k % QK8_0 == 0);
   275 |     const int nb = k / QK8_0;
   276 | 
   277 |     for (int i = 0; i < nb; i++) {
   278 |         float amax = 0.0f; // absolute max
   279 | 
   280 |         for (int j = 0; j < QK8_0; j++) {
   281 |             const float v = x[i*QK8_0 + j];
   282 |             amax = MAX(amax, fabsf(v));
   283 |         }
   284 | 
   285 |         const float d = amax / ((1 << 7) - 1);
   286 |         const float id = d ? 1.0f/d : 0.0f;
   287 | 
   288 |         y[i].d = GGML_FP32_TO_FP16(d);
   289 | 
   290 |         for (int j = 0; j < QK8_0; ++j) {
   291 |             const float x0 = x[i*QK8_0 + j]*id;
   292 | 
   293 |             y[i].qs[j] = roundf(x0);
   294 |         }
   295 |     }
  ----- lines 630-635 -----
   630 | static inline int nearest_int(float fval) {
   631 |     assert(fabsf(fval) <= 4194303.f);
   632 |     float val = fval + 12582912.f;
   633 |     int i; memcpy(&i, &val, sizeof(int));
   634 |     return (i & 0x007fffff) - 0x00400000;
   635 | }
  ----- lines 2784-2821 -----
  2784 | void quantize_row_q8_K_ref(const float * GGML_RESTRICT x, block_q8_K * GGML_RESTRICT y, int64_t k) {
  2785 |     assert(k % QK_K == 0);
  2786 |     const int64_t nb = k / QK_K;
  2787 | 
  2788 |     for (int i = 0; i < nb; i++) {
  2789 | 
  2790 |         float max = 0;
  2791 |         float amax = 0;
  2792 |         for (int j = 0; j < QK_K; ++j) {
  2793 |             float ax = fabsf(x[j]);
  2794 |             if (ax > amax) {
  2795 |                 amax = ax; max = x[j];
  2796 |             }
  2797 |         }
  2798 |         if (!amax) {
  2799 |             y[i].d = 0;
  2800 |             memset(y[i].qs, 0, QK_K);
  2801 |             x += QK_K;
  2802 |             continue;
  2803 |         }
  2804 |         //const float iscale = -128.f/max;
  2805 |         // We need this change for IQ2_XXS, else the AVX implementation becomes very awkward
  2806 |         const float iscale = -127.f/max;
  2807 |         for (int j = 0; j < QK_K; ++j) {
  2808 |             int v = nearest_int(iscale*x[j]);
  2809 |             y[i].qs[j] = MIN(127, v);
  2810 |         }
  2811 |         for (int j = 0; j < QK_K/16; ++j) {
  2812 |             int sum = 0;
  2813 |             for (int ii = 0; ii < 16; ++ii) {
  2814 |                 sum += y[i].qs[j*16 + ii];
  2815 |             }
  2816 |             y[i].bsums[j] = sum;
  2817 |         }
  2818 |         y[i].d = 1/iscale;
  2819 |         x += QK_K;
  2820 |     }
  2821 | }
【事実】CPU Q8_K: 256ごとに絶対最大に対応する符号付きmaxを選びiscale=-127/max、nearest_int(iscale*x)、int8、d=1/iscaleをfloatに格納、bsums=16個ごとのint16和。nearest_intは大きな定数へのFP32加算とビット抽出（通常丸めモード依存）；Q8_0は32ごと、amax/127をFP16へ保存、AVXのround-to-nearestと127/amax乗算を用いる。CUDAのroundf(値/d)と同一と保証されない。
【事実】候補CPU library nm -D: quantize_row_q8_Kと各IQ vec_dotおよび3 compact dot、xeon_mxfp4_rhs_*をexport。objdump quantize_row_q8_Kはquantize_row_q8_K_ref@pltへのjmp。

    78 | static int add_selected(void * opaque, const ggml_tensor * t, const uint32_t * selected, size_t selected_count, bool full_identity) {
    79 |     // 1 = added, 0 = ineligible/budget/duplicate, -1 = allocation or reserve failure.
    80 |     if (!opaque || !eligible(t) || t->ne[2] > 65536) return 0;
    81 |     try {
    82 |         auto & o = *static_cast<owner *>(opaque);
    83 |         const size_t experts = size_t(t->ne[2]);
    84 |         if (full_identity) selected_count = experts;
    85 |         if (!selected_count || selected_count > experts || (!full_identity && !selected)) return 0;
    86 |         const size_t original_blocks = ggml_nbytes(t)/ggml_type_size(t->type);
    87 |         if (original_blocks%experts) return 0;
    88 |         const size_t expert_blocks = original_blocks/experts;
    89 |         if (expert_blocks > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()/selected_count) return 0;
    90 |         const size_t blocks = expert_blocks*selected_count;
    91 |         if (blocks > (std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()-65536)/sizeof(compact_block)) return 0;
    92 |         std::vector<uint32_t> ids(selected_count);
    93 |         std::vector<uint8_t> seen(experts, 0);
    94 |         bool identity = selected_count == experts;
    95 |         for (size_t slot = 0; slot < selected_count; ++slot) {
    96 |             const uint32_t id = full_identity ? uint32_t(slot) : selected[slot];
    97 |             if (id >= experts || seen[id]) return 0;
    98 |             seen[id] = 1; ids[slot] = id;
    99 |             identity = identity && id == slot;
   100 |         }
   101 |         const long page = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
   102 |         if (page <= 0 || page > 65536) return -1;
   103 |         const size_t bytes = (blocks*sizeof(compact_block)+page-1)/page*page;
   104 |         std::unique_lock<std::mutex> lock(registry_mutex);
   105 |         if (registry.count(t->data) || bytes > o.budget-o.used || bytes > cap-allocated) return 0;
   106 |         const size_t available = available_memory();
   107 |         if (available < o.reserve || bytes > available-o.reserve) return -1;
   108 |         auto e = std::make_unique<entry>();
   109 |         e->source = t->data;
   110 |         e->type = t->type;
   111 |         e->bytes = bytes;
   112 |         std::memcpy(e->ne, t->ne, sizeof(e->ne));
   113 |         std::memcpy(e->nb, t->nb, sizeof(e->nb));
   114 |         e->expert_ptrs.resize(experts, nullptr);
   115 |         e->legacy_identity = identity;
   116 |         e->data = mmap(nullptr, bytes, PROT_READ|PROT_WRITE, MAP_PRIVATE|MAP_ANONYMOUS, -1, 0);

   121 |         registry.reserve(registry.size()+1);

   133 |                 } else {

   135 |                 }
   136 |             }
   137 |         }

    28 | template<int mode> static inline float mx_dot(int n,const block_mxfp4 * w,const block_q8_0 * x,const mx_rhs_view & rhs) {

    30 |     __m256 acc=_mm256_setzero_ps();int b=0;const int blocks=n/32;

    44 |         }
    45 |         acc=_mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(scales,values),acc);
    46 |     }
    47 |     float sum=mx_hsum(acc);
    48 |     // Keep the active reference's scalar odd-block tail, including multiplication order.
    49 |     for (;b<blocks;++b) {
    50 |         const float d=GGML_CPU_FP16_TO_FP32(x[b].d)*GGML_CPU_E8M0_TO_FP32_HALF(w[b].e);
    51 |         int s0=0,s1=0;
    52 |         for (int j=0;j<16;++j) {s0+=x[b].qs[j]*kvalues_fp4[w[b].qs[j]&15];s1+=x[b].qs[j+16]*kvalues_fp4[w[b].qs[j]>>4];}
    53 |         sum+=d*(s0+s1);
    54 |     }
    55 |     return sum;
    56 | }
【事実】MX sidecarは既存Q8_0からblock別scaleとbias=12の整数補正を計算してdotへ再使用。0x7c00系nonfiniteまたは-128を検出するとsafeが立たず既存dotに戻る。計算中の偏り補正であり入力のゼロ点が12に変更されたという意味ではない。

=== D. CUDA MMVQ、Q8_1、整数dotと縮約 ===
【事実】GPU量子化weight、F32 src1/dst、T=1（dst ne2=1）ならCUDA MUL_MAT_IDの先頭quantized MMVQ分岐条件が満たされる。MMVQはfloat入力全rowをQ8_1へ変換。downはSwiGLUのF32結果をあらためてQ8_1へ変換。src0 typeにかかわらず同じquantize_q8_1 kernel。
【事実】GPU Q8_1: 32要素のabs max/127、各xi/dをroundf、int8、scale dをFP16、原始入力の和をFP16へ格納。次のIQ2_S/IQ3_XXS/IQ3_S/MXFP4 MMVQ dotはds lowのみ読み、highのsum補正は使用しない。
【推定】IQのCPU Q8_K 256値で一つのF32符号付きscaleに対し、CUDA Q8_1は同じ256値で最大8個の正のFP16 scaleを用いる。これは表現の明白な差だが、最終誤差の各原因への寄与割合は既存資料では分離されていない。

原文: /home/codex/projects/llm-lab/exploration-20261112/cuda-sort/ggml-cuda.cu

    53 | __launch_bounds__(CUDA_QUANTIZE_BLOCK_SIZE, 1)
    54 | static __global__ void quantize_q8_1(
    55 |         const float * x_ptr, void * vy_ptr,
    56 |         const int64_t ne00, const int64_t s01, const int64_t s02, const int64_t s03,
    57 |         const int64_t ne0, const uint32_t ne1, const uint3 ne2) {
    58 |     ggml_cuda_pdl_lc();
    59 |     const float * GGML_CUDA_RESTRICT x  = x_ptr;
    60 |     void        * GGML_CUDA_RESTRICT vy = vy_ptr;
    61 |     const int64_t i0 = (int64_t)blockDim.x*blockIdx.x + threadIdx.x;
    62 | 
    63 |     if (i0 >= ne0) {
    64 |         return;
    65 |     }
    66 | 
    67 |     const int64_t i3 = fastdiv(blockIdx.z, ne2);
    68 |     const int64_t i2 = blockIdx.z - i3*ne2.z;
    69 |     const int64_t i1 = blockIdx.y;
    70 | 
    71 |     const int64_t & i00 = i0;
    72 |     const int64_t & i01 = i1;
    73 |     const int64_t & i02 = i2;
    74 |     const int64_t & i03 = i3;
    75 | 
    76 |     const int64_t i_cont = ((i3*ne2.z + i2) * ne1 + i1) * ne0 + i0;
    77 | 
    78 |     block_q8_1 * y = (block_q8_1 *) vy;
    79 | 
    80 |     const int64_t ib  = i_cont / QK8_1; // block index
    81 |     const int64_t iqs = i_cont % QK8_1; // quant index
    82 | 
    83 |     ggml_cuda_pdl_sync();
    84 |     const float xi = i0 < ne00 ? x[i03*s03 + i02*s02 + i01*s01 + i00] : 0.0f;
    85 |     float amax = fabsf(xi);
    86 |     float sum = xi;
    87 | 
    88 |     amax = warp_reduce_max<QK8_1>(amax);
    89 |     sum  = warp_reduce_sum<QK8_1>(sum);
    90 | 
    91 |     const float  d = amax / 127.0f;
    92 |     const int8_t q = amax == 0.0f ? 0 : roundf(xi / d);
    93 | 
    94 |     y[ib].qs[iqs] = q;
    95 | 
    96 |     if (iqs > 0) {
    97 |         return;
    98 |     }
    99 | 
   100 |     y[ib].ds = make_half2(d, sum);
   101 | }

   307 | #define VDR_MXFP4_Q8_1_MMVQ 2
   308 | #define VDR_MXFP4_Q8_1_MMQ  4
   309 | 
   310 | static __device__ __forceinline__ float vec_dot_mxfp4_q8_1(
   311 |     const void * __restrict__ vbq, const block_q8_1 * __restrict__ bq8_1, const int & kbx, const int & iqs) {
   312 | 
   313 |     const block_mxfp4 * bq4 = (const block_mxfp4 *) vbq + kbx;
   314 | 
   315 |     const int * q8 = (const int *) bq8_1->qs + iqs;
   316 | 
   317 |     int sumi = 0;
   318 | #pragma unroll
   319 |     for (int l = 0; l < VDR_MXFP4_Q8_1_MMVQ; ++l) {
   320 |         const int aux_q4 = get_int_b1(bq4->qs, iqs + l);
   321 |         const int2 v = get_int_from_table_16(aux_q4, kvalues_mxfp4);

   325 |     }

  1184 | #define VDR_IQ2_S_Q8_1_MMVQ 2
  1185 | #define VDR_IQ2_S_Q8_1_MMQ  2
  1186 | 
  1187 | static __device__ __forceinline__ float vec_dot_iq2_s_q8_1(
  1188 |     const void * __restrict__ vbq, const block_q8_1 * __restrict__ bq8_1, const int & kbx, const int & iqs) {
  1189 | 
  1190 |     const block_iq2_s * bq2 = (const block_iq2_s *) vbq + kbx;
  1191 | 
  1192 |     const int       qs_packed = get_int_b2(bq2->qs, iqs/2);
  1193 |     const uint8_t * qs        = (const uint8_t *) &qs_packed;
  1194 | 
  1195 |     const int qh = bq2->qh[iqs/2];
  1196 | 
  1197 |     const int       signs_packed_32 = get_int_b2(bq2->qs, QK_K/32 + iqs/2);
  1198 |     const uint8_t * signs_packed_8  = (const uint8_t *) &signs_packed_32;
  1199 | 
  1200 |     const int ls0 = bq2->scales[iqs/2] & 0x0F;
  1201 |     const int ls1 = bq2->scales[iqs/2] >> 4;
  1202 | 
  1203 |     int sumi0 = 0;
  1204 |     int sumi1 = 0;
  1205 | #pragma unroll
  1206 |     for (int l0 = 0; l0 < 8; l0 += 2) {
  1207 |         const int * grid_pos = (const int *)(iq2s_grid + (qs[l0/2] | ((qh << (8-l0)) & 0x300)));
  1208 | 
  1209 |         const int signs0 = __vcmpne4(((signs_packed_8[l0/2] & 0x03) << 7) | ((signs_packed_8[l0/2] & 0x0C) << 21), 0x00000000);
  1210 |         const int signs1 = __vcmpne4(((signs_packed_8[l0/2] & 0x30) << 3) | ((signs_packed_8[l0/2] & 0xC0) << 17), 0x00000000);
  1211 | 
  1212 |         const int grid_l = __vsub4(grid_pos[0] ^ signs0, signs0);
  1213 |         const int grid_h = __vsub4(grid_pos[1] ^ signs1, signs1);
  1214 | 
  1215 |         const int u0 = get_int_b4(bq8_1[iqs/2].qs, l0 + 0);
  1216 |         const int u1 = get_int_b4(bq8_1[iqs/2].qs, l0 + 1);
  1217 | 
  1218 |         if (l0 < 4) {
  1219 |             sumi0 = ggml_cuda_dp4a(grid_l, u0, sumi0);
  1220 |             sumi0 = ggml_cuda_dp4a(grid_h, u1, sumi0);
  1221 |         } else {
  1222 |             sumi1 = ggml_cuda_dp4a(grid_l, u0, sumi1);
  1223 |             sumi1 = ggml_cuda_dp4a(grid_h, u1, sumi1);
  1224 |         }
  1225 |     }
  1226 |     const int sumi = (sumi0*ls0 + sumi1*ls1 + (sumi0 + sumi1)/2)/4;
  1227 | 
  1228 |     const float d = __half2float(bq2->d) * __low2float(bq8_1[iqs/2].ds);
  1229 |     return d * sumi;
  1230 | }
  1231 | 
  1232 | #define VDR_IQ3_XXS_Q8_1_MMVQ 2
  1233 | #define VDR_IQ3_XXS_Q8_1_MMQ  2
  1234 | 
  1235 | static __device__ __forceinline__ float vec_dot_iq3_xxs_q8_1(
  1236 |     const void * __restrict__ vbq, const block_q8_1 * __restrict__ bq8_1, const int & kbx, const int & iqs) {
  1237 | 
  1238 |     const block_iq3_xxs * bq3 = (const block_iq3_xxs *) vbq + kbx;
  1239 | 
  1240 |     const int2 q3_packed = make_int2(get_int_b2(bq3->qs, iqs), get_int_b2(bq3->qs, iqs+1));
  1241 |     const uint8_t * q3 = (const uint8_t *) &q3_packed;
  1242 |     const uint32_t aux32 = get_int_b2(bq3->qs, QK_K/16 + iqs/2);
  1243 | 
  1244 |     int sumi = 0;
  1245 | #pragma unroll
  1246 |     for (int l0 = 0; l0 < 8; l0 += 2) {
  1247 |         const int2 grid_pos = make_int2(iq3xxs_grid[q3[l0 + 0]], iq3xxs_grid[q3[l0 + 1]]);
  1248 |         const uint32_t signs = unpack_ksigns(aux32 >> (7*l0/2));
  1249 | 
  1250 |         const int signs0 = __vcmpne4(signs & 0x08040201, 0);
  1251 |         const int grid_l = __vsub4(grid_pos.x ^ signs0, signs0);
  1252 | 
  1253 |         const int u0 = get_int_b4(bq8_1[iqs/2].qs, l0 + 0);
  1254 | 
  1255 |         const int signs1 = __vcmpne4(signs & 0x80402010, 0);
  1256 |         const int grid_h = __vsub4(grid_pos.y ^ signs1, signs1);
  1257 | 
  1258 |         const int u1 = get_int_b4(bq8_1[iqs/2].qs, l0 + 1);
  1259 | 
  1260 |         sumi = ggml_cuda_dp4a(grid_l, u0, sumi);
  1261 |         sumi = ggml_cuda_dp4a(grid_h, u1, sumi);
  1262 |     }
  1263 | 
  1264 |     const int ls = aux32 >> 28;
  1265 |     sumi = (ls*sumi + sumi/2)/2;
  1266 |     const float d = __half2float(bq3->d) * __low2float(bq8_1[iqs/2].ds);
  1267 |     return d * sumi;
  1268 | }
  1269 | 
  1270 | #define VDR_IQ3_S_Q8_1_MMVQ 2
  1271 | #define VDR_IQ3_S_Q8_1_MMQ  2
  1272 | 
  1273 | // TODO: don't use lookup table for signs
  1274 | static __device__ __forceinline__ float vec_dot_iq3_s_q8_1(
  1275 |     const void * __restrict__ vbq, const block_q8_1 * __restrict__ bq8_1, const int & kbx, const int & iqs) {
  1276 | 
  1277 |     const block_iq3_s * bq3 = (const block_iq3_s *) vbq + kbx;
  1278 | 
  1279 |     const int2      qs_packed = make_int2(get_int_b2(bq3->qs, iqs + 0), get_int_b2(bq3->qs, iqs + 1));
  1280 |     const uint8_t * qs        = (const uint8_t *) &qs_packed;
  1281 | 
  1282 |     const int qh = bq3->qh[iqs/2];
  1283 | 
  1284 |     const int       signs_packed_32 = get_int_b2(bq3->signs, iqs/2);
  1285 |     const uint8_t * signs_packed_8  = (const uint8_t *) &signs_packed_32;
  1286 | 
  1287 |     int sumi = 0;
  1288 | #pragma unroll
  1289 |     for (int l0 = 0; l0 < 8; l0 += 2) {
  1290 |         const int2 grid_pos = make_int2(
  1291 |             iq3s_grid[qs[l0 + 0] | ((qh << (8 - l0)) & 0x100)],
  1292 |             iq3s_grid[qs[l0 + 1] | ((qh << (7 - l0)) & 0x100)]);
  1293 | 
  1294 |         const int signs0 = __vcmpne4(((signs_packed_8[l0/2] & 0x03) << 7) | ((signs_packed_8[l0/2] & 0x0C) << 21), 0x00000000);
  1295 |         const int signs1 = __vcmpne4(((signs_packed_8[l0/2] & 0x30) << 3) | ((signs_packed_8[l0/2] & 0xC0) << 17), 0x00000000);
  1296 | 
  1297 |         const int grid_l = __vsub4(grid_pos.x ^ signs0, signs0);
  1298 |         const int grid_h = __vsub4(grid_pos.y ^ signs1, signs1);
  1299 | 
  1300 |         const int u0 = get_int_b4(bq8_1[iqs/2].qs, l0 + 0);
  1301 |         const int u1 = get_int_b4(bq8_1[iqs/2].qs, l0 + 1);
  1302 | 
  1303 |         sumi = ggml_cuda_dp4a(grid_l, u0, sumi);
  1304 |         sumi = ggml_cuda_dp4a(grid_h, u1, sumi);
  1305 |     }
  1306 | 
  1307 |     sumi *= 1 + 2*((bq3->scales[iqs/4] >> ((iqs << 1) & 0x04)) & 0x0F);
  1308 | 
  1309 |     const float d = __half2float(bq3->d) * __low2float(bq8_1[iqs/2].ds);
  1310 |     return d * sumi;
  1311 | }
【事実】各vec_dotは圧縮weightをtable/gridと符号で復元し、int8x4のdp4aをint累積、FP16 weight scale x Q8_1 FP16 scaleでfloat結果を返す。IQ2_SとIQ3_XXSにはC++整数除算があり、floatでの1/8,1/4後乗算と丸め順が必ず一致するとは限らない。量子化粒度以外の別候補だが最終差寄与は未測定。IQ3_Sは奇数scaleで整数乗算。
【事実】MMVQ T=1 kernelはスレッドごとfloat部分和、warp間sharedメモリ加算、warp_reduce_sumで縮約。CPU compactは8 FP32 laneの積和と水平和。順番は同一ではない。
【事実】CUDAのgate/up type/shape/stride/input/idsが一致しSWIGLU_CLAMP等の条件を満たすとfusion候補。GPU private Graphのgate/upの型はplanの各layer参照。静的コードの候補判定だけでは実行時にfusionが起きたことは確証できない。fusionするとQ8_1入力を共有し両dotの結果からclamp/SiLUを一kernelで出力。

  3544 | // ggml_compute_forward_swiglu_clamp
  3545 | 
  3546 | static void ggml_compute_forward_swiglu_clamp_f32(const ggml_compute_params * params, ggml_tensor * dst) {
  3547 |     const ggml_tensor * src0   = dst->src[0];
  3548 |     const ggml_tensor * src1   = dst->src[1];
  3549 |     char *              src0_d = (char *) src0->data;
  3550 |     char *              src1_d = (char *) (src1 ? src1->data : src0->data);
  3551 |     const size_t        src0_o = src0->nb[1];
  3552 |     const size_t        src1_o = src1 ? src1->nb[1] : src0->nb[1];
  3553 | 
  3554 |     GGML_ASSERT(ggml_is_contiguous_1(src0));
  3555 |     GGML_ASSERT(ggml_is_contiguous_1(dst));
  3556 | 
  3557 |     if (src1) {
  3558 |         GGML_ASSERT(ggml_is_contiguous_1(src1));
  3559 |         GGML_ASSERT(src0->type == src1->type);
  3560 |     }
  3561 | 
  3562 |     const int ith = params->ith;
  3563 |     const int nth = params->nth;
  3564 | 
  3565 |     const int nc = src1 ? src0->ne[0] : src0->ne[0] / 2;
  3566 |     const int nr = ggml_nrows(src0);
  3567 | 
  3568 |     GGML_ASSERT(dst->ne[0] == nc);
  3569 |     GGML_ASSERT(ggml_nrows(dst) == nr);
  3570 | 
  3571 |     const int32_t swapped = ggml_get_op_params_i32(dst, 1);
  3572 |     const float   limit   = ggml_get_op_params_f32(dst, 3);
  3573 | 
  3574 |     const int dr  = (nr + nth - 1) / nth;
  3575 |     const int ir0 = dr * ith;
  3576 |     const int ir1 = MIN(ir0 + dr, nr);
  3577 | 
  3578 |     for (int i1 = ir0; i1 < ir1; i1++) {
  3579 |         float * src0_p = (float *) (src0_d + i1 * src0_o);
  3580 |         float * src1_p = (float *) (src1_d + i1 * src1_o);
  3581 |         float * dst_p  = (float *) ((char *) dst->data + i1 * (dst->nb[1]));
  3582 | 
  3583 |         if (!src1) {
  3584 |             src0_p += swapped ? nc : 0;
  3585 |             src1_p += swapped ? 0 : nc;
  3586 |         }
  3587 | 
  3588 |         for (int k = 0; k < nc; k++) {
  3589 |             const float gate = std::min(src0_p[k], limit);
  3590 |             const float up   = std::clamp(src1_p[k], -limit, limit);
  3591 |             dst_p[k]         = gate / (1.f + expf(-gate)) * up;
  3592 |         }
  3593 | 
  3594 | #ifndef NDEBUG
  3595 |         for (int k = 0; k < nc; k++) {
  3596 |             const float x = dst_p[k];
  3597 |             GGML_UNUSED(x);
  3598 |             assert(!isnan(x));
  3599 |             assert(!isinf(x));
  3600 |         }
  3601 | #endif  // NDEBUG
  3602 |     }
  3603 | }

   483 | // swiglu_clamp
   484 | 
   485 | template <typename T>
   486 | static __global__ void swiglu_clamp_kernel(const T * gate, const T * up, T * dst, const int64_t k, const int64_t n, const int64_t o0, const int64_t o1, float limit) {
   487 |     const int64_t i = int64_t(blockDim.x)*blockIdx.x + threadIdx.x;
   488 | 
   489 |     if (i >= k) {
   490 |         return;
   491 |     }
   492 | 
   493 |     const int64_t j0 = (i / n) * o0 + (i % n);
   494 |     const int64_t j1 = o0 == o1 ? j0 : (i / n) * o1 + (i % n);
   495 | 
   496 |     dst[i] = (T) ggml_cuda_op_swiglu_clamp_single((float) gate[j0], (float) up[j1], limit);
   497 | }
   498 | 
   499 | template <typename T>
   500 | static void swiglu_clamp_cuda(const T * gate, const T * up, T * dst, const int64_t k, const int64_t n, const int64_t o0, const int64_t o1, const float limit, cudaStream_t stream) {
   501 |     const int64_t num_blocks = (k + CUDA_GLU_BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / CUDA_GLU_BLOCK_SIZE;
   502 |     swiglu_clamp_kernel<<<num_blocks, CUDA_GLU_BLOCK_SIZE, 0, stream>>>(gate, up, dst, k, n, o0, o1, limit);
   503 | }
   504 | 
【事実】CPU/GPUの式はgate=min(gate,limit), up=clamp(up,-limit,limit), gate/(1+expf(-gate))*up。CUDAはnvcc -use_fast_mathを使用しており、CPUのexpfとbit一致は保証されない。CUDAの機械命令や誤差はここでは未測定。

【事実】元のggml-backend.cpp.oのみoverlayへ差し替え、ggml-quants.c.oをdeepseek buildのままリンク。deepseek source /ggml-quants.c SHA256 bcda33625707fdfcb1367cdd2bb1a2f017430162ec9623abc84ad17459698f73 はCPU hybrid sourceの同ファイルと同一。nm -D gpu-stream base はquantize_row_q8_K_refを0000000000087770でexport、candidate CPUのquantize_row_q8_Kはref@pltへjmp。再配置やsymbol interpositionなど動的挙動の全実証はしていない。
【事実】先に掲載したdeepseek sourceのggml-backend.cppは位置決定の一般形。稼働候補baseの変更済み同関数は次のgpu-stream側が正。追加されたoffload条件はMXFP4 MUL_MAT_IDでsrc1 ne[2]が指定threshold以上かどうか。

  1328 | // returns the backend that should be used for the node based on the current locations
  1329 | static bool ggml_backend_sched_offload_batch_ok(const ggml_tensor * tensor) {
  1330 |     static const int64_t min_mxfp4 = []() -> int64_t {
  1331 |         const char * text = getenv("GGML_SCHED_MXFP4_OFFLOAD_MIN_BATCH");
  1332 |         if (!text || !text[0]) return 0;
  1333 |         char * end = nullptr;
  1334 |         const long value = strtol(text, &end, 10);
  1335 |         return end && !*end && value >= 1 && value <= 4096 ? value : 0;

  1338 |         tensor->src[1]->ne[2] >= min_mxfp4;
  1339 | }

  1347 |     }

  1352 |         if (cur_backend_id != -1) {
  1353 |             SET_CAUSE(tensor, "1.vsrc");

  ----- lines 572-591 -----
   572 | // GGML_CPU_TILED_MM: master switch, on by default. If off, we fast return false and normal vec_dot mul_mat resumes
   573 | static bool ggml_tiled_matmul_enabled(void) {
   574 |     static bool enabled = true;
   575 |     static std::once_flag flag;
   576 |     std::call_once(flag, []() {
   577 |         const char * env = getenv("GGML_CPU_TILED_MM");
   578 |         enabled = env == NULL || atoi(env) != 0;
   579 |     });
   580 |     return enabled;
   581 | }
   582 | 
   583 | // GGML_CPU_TILED_MM_FORCE: test/bench only, take the tiled path even when unprofitable
   584 | static bool ggml_tiled_matmul_forced(void) {
   585 |     static bool forced = false;
   586 |     static std::once_flag flag;
   587 |     std::call_once(flag, []() {
   588 |         const char * env = getenv("GGML_CPU_TILED_MM_FORCE");
   589 |         forced = env != NULL && atoi(env) == 1;
   590 |     });
   591 |     return forced;
  ----- lines 594-643 -----
   594 | // hard constraints shared by the MUL_MAT and MUL_MAT_ID entries; the src0 type gate is the
   595 | // entries' type switch
   596 | 
   597 | #if !defined(__AVX512VNNI__) && !defined(__AVX2__) && !defined(__AVX__)
   598 | static bool ggml_tiled_supported(const struct ggml_tensor * src0,
   599 |                                  const struct ggml_tensor * src1) {
   600 |     UNUSED(src0);
   601 |     UNUSED(src1);
   602 |     return false;
   603 | }
   604 | #else
   605 | static bool ggml_tiled_supported(const struct ggml_tensor * src0,
   606 |                                  const struct ggml_tensor * src1) {
   607 |     if (!ggml_tiled_matmul_enabled()) {
   608 |         return false;
   609 |     }
   610 | 
   611 |     // repack-buffer weights hold a repacked layout, let that kernel handle
   612 |     if (src0->extra != NULL) {
   613 |         return false;
   614 |     }
   615 |     // Supported quant types for src0
   616 |     switch (src0->type) {
   617 |         case GGML_TYPE_Q6_K:
   618 |         case GGML_TYPE_Q5_K:
   619 |         case GGML_TYPE_Q4_K:
   620 |         case GGML_TYPE_Q3_K:
   621 |         case GGML_TYPE_Q2_K:
   622 |         case GGML_TYPE_IQ4_XS:
   623 |         case GGML_TYPE_IQ2_XXS:
   624 |         case GGML_TYPE_IQ2_XS:
   625 |         case GGML_TYPE_IQ2_S:
   626 |         case GGML_TYPE_IQ3_XXS:
   627 |         case GGML_TYPE_IQ3_S:
   628 |         case GGML_TYPE_IQ1_S:
   629 |         case GGML_TYPE_IQ1_M:
   630 |             return true;
   631 |         default:
   632 |             return false;
   633 |     }
   634 |     if (src1->type != GGML_TYPE_F32 && src1->type != GGML_TYPE_Q8_K) {
   635 |         return false;
   636 |     }
   637 | 
   638 |     if (src1->type == GGML_TYPE_Q8_K && !ggml_is_contiguous(src1)) {
   639 |         // We can handle noncontiguous floats because we're repacking to q8_k anyways
   640 |         return false;
   641 |     }
   642 |     return true;
   643 | }

  1202 | static bool ggml_tiled_min_batch(int64_t rows) {
  1203 |     //  Profitable at rows >= 8, take even when unprofitable if we're forced
  1204 |     return rows >= 8 || ggml_tiled_matmul_forced();
  1205 | }
  ----- lines 1225-1245 -----
  1225 | // MUL_MAT_ID (MoE), one expert; returns true if the expert was computed here,
  1226 | // per expert eligibility (type gate, batch floor) is decided here
  1227 | bool ggml_compute_forward_mul_mat_id_tiled(
  1228 |         const struct ggml_compute_params * params,
  1229 |               struct ggml_tensor *         dst,
  1230 |         int64_t                            cur_a,
  1231 |         int64_t                            cne1,
  1232 |         const int32_t *                    expert_rows,
  1233 |         char *                             scratch) {
  1234 |     if (params->use_ref) {
  1235 |         return false;
  1236 |     }
  1237 |     if (!ggml_tiled_supported(dst->src[0], dst->src[1])) {
  1238 |         return false;
  1239 |     }
  1240 |     // profitability is per expert: the rows routed to this expert
  1241 |     if (!ggml_tiled_min_batch(cne1)) {
  1242 |         return false;
  1243 |     }
  1244 |     return ggml_tiled_matmul_type_dispatch(params, dst, expert_rows, cur_a, cne1, scratch);
  1245 | }
【推定・条件明記】private CPUはID一意性をrequireで検証し、T=1は各expertへのcne1が0または1。したがってGGML_CPU_TILED_MM_FORCEが1でなければcne1>=8を満たさず、noncompact IQはtiledでなくtype_traits vec_dotへfallback。MXFP4はggml_tiled_supportedのtype switchに存在しない。final環境辞書と生成scriptにFORCE指定は見当たらないが、環境変数の親プロセスからの継承の有無をこの調査で実測していない。
【事実】hot-14GiB.planの全40行を型と選択expert数のみ抽出（順にgate/up/down、ID一覧省略）:

【事実】layer37,38,39のselected=0。CPU plan先頭=XEON_IQ_CACHE_PLAN_V2 87 16798 111463464960。prepare_hot_plans.py 51-58行の設計は元selected 19200からoverlapを除外し未選択状態を37-39へ追加、補充なし。final vs hot14には3層GPU->CPUとcompact再構成が同時に存在し、熱いexpert計算単独のCPU/GPU差で原因を限定できない。
【事実】同一hot libraryでGPU resident=0、元37-39 GPUを保持するdisabled vs all-miss CPUのABBA結果（MISS_ABBA.json lines 2, 120-138）では8入力各128 IDは一致、aggregate decode 7.575479465870688 vs 7.4936905117324715 tok/s、gain -1.0796538292618396%。これはprovider挿入・all-missに関する範囲で、hot14で同時に変更された37-39の移動・cache再編・GPU hitを独立検証していない。

    52 |             assert len(warm['tokens'])==1 and warm['timings']['cache_n']==0 and warm['timings']['prompt_n']==length and warm['tokens_cached']==length
    53 |             row={'name':case['name'],'warm_timings':warm['timings'],'points':[]}
    54 |             for j in range(a.steps):
    55 |                 start=time.monotonic();reply=completion({'prompt':prompt+known[:j+1],'n_predict':1,'n_probs':100,'cache_prompt':True});wall=time.monotonic()-start
    56 |                 t=reply['timings'];assert len(reply['tokens'])==t['predicted_n']==1 and t['cache_n']==length+j and t['prompt_n']==1 and reply['tokens_cached']==length+j+1
    57 |                 assert reply['stop_type']=='limit'
    58 |                 probabilities=reply.get('completion_probabilities') or []
    59 |                 assert len(probabilities)<=1
    60 |                 if probabilities:assert probabilities[0]['id']==reply['tokens'][0]
    61 |                 row['points'].append({'j':j,'known_next_id':known[j+1],'generated_id':reply['tokens'][0],'matches_frozen_baseline':reply['tokens'][0]==known[j+1],'probability':probabilities[0] if probabilities else None,'timings':t,'wall_s':wall})
【事実】probe FP64参照は既存量子化重みをto_floatでdequantizeした値を使い、F64 dotの各gate/up/down結果をF32化、SwiGLUもF32。元unquantized modelの完全FP64基準ではない。test-backendの閾値NMSE<1e-2はfixtureの数値診断、実回答品質基準ではない。既存NUMERIC証拠は同ディレクトリEXISTING_NUMERIC_EVIDENCE.json。
【補足・未確認】CUDA IQ2_S/IQ3_XXS関数に整数型の除算演算子があることは原文で確認済み。実weight値、gridの偶奇、入力を考慮したとき切り捨てが本測定で実際に発生したか、もしくは推論差をどれだけ説明するかは未確認。
